[00:48:57.534] And whereas the members of this chamber join with the residents of Chicago, the
[00:49:01.214] nation, and the global community in honoring the life and legacy of the
[00:49:05.094] Reverend Jesse Louis Jackson Sr., one of the most consequential

[00:49:12.534] vision, and organizing power helped reshape the democratic and social
[00:49:16.274] landscape of the United States. And whereas the Reverend Jesse Louis Jackson
[00:49:20.194] Sr. was born Jesse Louis Burns on October 8th,
[00:49:23.414] 1941 in Greenville, South Carolina, later taking the
[00:49:27.294] surname of his stepfather, Charles Henry Jackson.
[00:49:30.324] And through faith-discipline, and extraordinary resolve, rose from humble
[00:49:34.382] beginnings to become a defining moral and political force of his
[00:49:37.422] generation. And whereas emerging from the tradition of prophetic, prophetic
[00:49:41.382] Black leadership and guided by an unwavering commitment to justice and human
[00:49:44.942] dignity, the Reverend Jackson translated the ethos of the civil rights
[00:49:48.642] movement into an enduring and evolving struggle for economic
[00:49:52.402] equity, political representation, educa-educational
[00:49:55.882] opportunity, and global human rights.
[00:49:58.582] And whereas the Reverend Jackson's l- Jackson's leadership and moral vision were
[00:50:02.222] profoundly shaped by the legacy and teachings of the Reverend Dr.
[00:50:05.702] Martin Luther King Jr., under whose guidance he emerged as a national figure,
[00:50:09.922] carrying forward the enduring principles of non-violence, justice, and
[00:50:13.782] human dignity that defined the civil rights movement.
[00:50:16.842] And whereas the Reverend Jackson rose to national prominence through his work
[00:50:19.962] alongside Reverend Dr. Martin Luther King Jr., yet it was the city of

[00:50:30.162] leadership. And whereas in Chicago, a city long defined by both its
[00:50:34.202] challenges and its possibilities, the Reverend Jackson built

[00:50:41.162] rooted in moral conviction, strategic coalition-building, and an
[00:50:44.642] unyielding belief in the transformative power of civic engagement.
[00:50:48.482] And whereas within the city of Chicago's historic South Side, and notably within
[00:50:52.282] the 4th Ward, the Reverend Jackson established Operation PUSH, People
[00:50:56.362] United to Save Humanity, an institution whose presence would be-
[00:51:00.182] become synonymous with empowerment, opportunity, and advocacy for
